# Revision 2737 - Soul_Assassin

^ Decals (and test bed on mig-29 temporarily):

+ passing a style for each placement array
['Number', [places1, places2], ['style1','style2']]
so same number can be expressed in various styles in various places
+ random style for one placement
['Number',  places, ['style1','style2']]
+ provide an array of static numbers to chose from randomly
['Number',  places, style, [23,24,25,34,45]]
so number will be randomly chosen from that list
the third one can be used in combi with the first two
+ combination of 1 and 2
['Number', [places1, places2], [['style1','style3'],'style2']]
places one will get random style from style 1 and 3 and places2 will always have style2
